# Morshidi — Codex Progress Record

## Student iOS-style system typography (2026-10-21, implemented and verified)

**Objective/slice:** Visual-only typography update for the full student route group: native Apple system fonts remain first on Apple devices, with self-hosted free Inter for Latin/numerals and IBM Plex Sans Arabic for Arabic elsewhere. Existing font sizes, declared weights, spacing, letter-spacing, data, routes, authentication, authorization, and business logic were preserved.

- **Starting branch/SHA:** `main` / `37e07a908a3e50b8ed30ec453e5715222d8ae65f`.
- **Initial worktree:** clean (`git status --short` returned no entries); local `main` matched `origin/main`.
- **Architecture verified before editing:** `docs/CODEX_PROGRESS.md`, `apps/web/AGENTS.md`, local Next.js 16.3.5 font documentation, root/student layouts, `globals.css`, student shell/dashboard components, font-weight usage across student TSX/CSS, relevant tests, and current Git state.

**Implementation details:**
1. Reused the existing `next/font/google` IBM Plex Sans Arabic instance at the root and renamed its generated variable to `--font-plex-arabic`, preserving the actual free, self-hosted weights 400/500/600/700 with `subsets: ["arabic", "latin"]` and `display: "swap"`. The existing global `--font-body` now aliases that variable, so non-student typography is unchanged.
2. Added a student-layout-scoped Inter instance with `--font-inter`, `display: "swap"`, Latin subset, and only the weights exercised by student styles: 400/500/600/700/800/900. The 900 file is required by existing `font-black` declarations; no typography sizes or declared weights were changed.
3. Added exactly one student stack token in `globals.css`: `--font-ui: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "SF Pro Arabic", "SF Pro Display", var(--font-inter), var(--font-plex-arabic), "Segoe UI", Tahoma, sans-serif;`. No Apple font file is bundled. The token is defined where the student Inter variable exists, avoiding invalid custom-property resolution.
4. Applied `--font-ui` to the student layout root and `.app-shell`, including protected-boundary states. Student buttons, inputs, selects, textareas, summaries, keyboard hints, badges/chips, and existing `.font-mono` chips inherit the same UI stack.
5. Applied `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` across the student workspace. Existing `letter-spacing` rules remain untouched. Browser verification confirmed shell and control stacks match, metrics resolve to `tabular-nums`, and Arabic/Latin content has no horizontal overflow at either requested viewport.
6. IBM Plex Sans Arabic has no official 800 face: Next.js reports its supported weights as 100–700 and rejected an 800 configuration during the first visual compile. The genuine requested 400–700 files are loaded; existing Arabic `font-weight: 800` declarations remain intact and use browser synthesis, while Latin/numerals use Inter's genuine 800 face. No nonexistent or mislabeled font binary was bundled.
7. Added a focused source-contract regression test for font variables, subsets, weights, swap behavior, inherited controls/chips, and tabular numerals. Captured and visually inspected deterministic before/after dashboard screenshots at 1280x900 and 390x844. The temporary synthetic capture route and temporary Playwright tooling were removed afterward.

- `apps/web/src/app/(student)/layout.tsx` - 842.
- `apps/web/src/app/(student)/typography.test.ts` - 1,538.
- `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` - 67,071.
- `docs/screenshots/ios-typography/before-1280.png` - 631,228.
- `docs/screenshots/ios-typography/after-1280.png` - 654,971.
- `docs/screenshots/ios-typography/before-390.png` - 219,958.

## Student Apple emoji rendering (2026-10-20, implemented and verified)

**Objective/slice:** Visual-only replacement of native emoji glyphs in the student shell and dashboard with locally hosted Apple 64px PNG artwork on every device. Data fetching, routes, authentication, authorization, academic calculations, deterministic rules, and business logic were kept unchanged.

- **Starting branch/SHA:** `main` / `e708a4a0eff5c07e5d9c649b456b9563dd7be6a8`.
- **Initial worktree:** clean (`git status --short` returned no entries).
- **Architecture verified before editing:** `docs/CODEX_PROGRESS.md`, `apps/web/AGENTS.md`, current Git state, frontend package scripts and test setup, the shared `AppShell`, `/student`, `StatCard`, related tests, and all TSX source under the student route/UI scan boundary.

**Implementation details:**
1. Added `emoji-datasource-apple@^16.0.0` as a development dependency and the repeatable `npm run emoji:copy` command. `scripts/copy-emoji.mjs` scans the student pages, shared shell, and UI components; resolves grapheme clusters through the package's `emoji.json`; handles qualified/unqualified variation-selector names; removes stale generated PNGs; and copies only the Apple `img-apple-64` files actually referenced by the UI.
2. Generated exactly 32 PNGs under `apps/web/public/emoji/` (174,220 bytes total): `1f30d`, `1f393`, `1f3af`, `1f3db-fe0f`, `1f3e0`, `1f44b`, `1f464`, `1f489`, `1f4a1`, `1f4c8`, `1f4ca`, `1f4cb`, `1f4d6`, `1f4da`, `1f4dc`, `1f4dd`, `1f504`, `1f514`, `1f52c`, `1f552`, `1f5d3-fe0f`, `1f5fa-fe0f`, `1f916`, `1f9d1-200d-1f393`, `1f9e9`, `1f9ed`, `1fa7a`, `23f3`, `26a1`, `2705`, `2728`, and `2b50`.
3. Added client component `Emoji` and helper `renderEmoji(text)`. They normalize NFC/code points, variation selectors, and ZWJ sequences; render a local lazy, non-draggable inline image at `1.1em` with `vertical-align: -0.15em`; preserve the original character as alt text when meaningful; support `aria-hidden` for decorative use; and fall back to the native grapheme when an asset fails.
4. Replaced directly rendered emoji in the desktop/mobile student shell, dashboard headings/actions/cards/chips, and `StatCard` string titles. Existing emoji data remains unchanged and is passed through `Emoji`/`renderEmoji`, preserving layout, copy, navigation, handlers, and state behavior.
5. Added focused component coverage for variation-selector and ZWJ normalization, rendered local image paths/decorative accessibility, and native-character fallback after an image error. Updated shell assertions to verify Apple asset paths.
6. Captured and visually inspected before/after screenshots at 1280x900 and 390x844 with deterministic synthetic student data. Temporary capture routing/tooling was removed afterward.

## Student Dashboard “Midnight Glass” visual restyle (2026-10-20, implemented and verified)

**Objective/slice:** Visual-only restyle of `/student` and its shared student shell for an Arabic RTL, iOS-inspired dark liquid-glass experience at desktop and phone widths. Data fetching, API paths, authentication, authorization, routes, academic calculations, deterministic decision boundaries, and real-data copy/value sources were not changed.

- **Starting branch/SHA:** `main` / `7a43a99870754790a7f1d5012945f965e102a6a6`.
- **Initial worktree:** clean (`git status --short` returned no entries).
- **Architecture verified before editing:** `apps/web/AGENTS.md`, Next.js 16.3.5 local viewport documentation, shared `AppShell`, `/student` client page, `StatCard`, `ProgressBar`, global token/CSS layers, dashboard/shell tests, frontend package scripts, and current Git state.

**Implementation details:**
1. Added the requested global Midnight Glass variables (`--text`, `--accent`, `--accent-text`, glass surface/border/blur/shadow, card radius, progress track, and one replaceable `--wallpaper` variable), fixed colorful desktop/mobile gradients, blur fallbacks, reduced-transparency handling, reduced-motion compatibility, safe-area spacing, tap-highlight suppression, and 44px-or-larger primary touch targets.
2. Restyled the shared student shell with a fixed 210px RTL glass sidebar, gold active destination, preserved 15 real navigation routes, requested native emoji mapping, glass notification/account controls, user chip, mobile drawer, and a four-item floating phone tab bar (`الرئيسية`, `التقدم`, `مرشدي`, `المواد`).
3. Restyled `/student` without changing its requests or decisions: responsive greeting/actions, glass subnavigation, governed Student Intelligence quiz banner, one hero plan-hours card plus four desktop/two-by-two metrics, phone hero plus three compact metrics, real-data progress indicators, glass profile/progress/action sections, and three-column/stacked requirement cards. `StatCard` retained all existing props and gained rendering support for its already-declared subtitle plus optional children/React-node values needed for the real hero progress presentation.
4. Added a student-segment static Next.js viewport with `themeColor: #05060b` and `viewportFit: "cover"` through the supported `Viewport` export, leaving the non-student viewport unchanged. The existing product accent is likewise preserved outside `.app-shell` despite the requested token being declared on `:root`.
5. Added focused regression assertions for glass presentation hooks and emoji navigation while preserving existing semantic/data assertions.
6. Captured baseline and final views from the exact starting SHA/current worktree using the same isolated fake authenticated session and deterministic mock academic dataset. Temporary capture route/tooling and the detached baseline worktree were removed afterward. Screenshot dimensions were verified from disk: 1280×900 and 390×844 for both states.

## First University Pilot Package + Onboarding System + Pilot Acceptance Workflow (2026-10-20, ready for human approval)

**Objective/slice:** Make the FIRST REAL UNIVERSITY PILOT operationally executable through a deterministic pilot onboarding system, lifecycle state machine, IT requirement tracker, blocker management, 12-criteria write-enablement fail-closed gate, acceptance check engine, multi-role signoffs, pilot package documents, and downloadable templates.
- Phase Goal: Controlled configuration/integration process for onboarding the first university pilot without custom code rewrites.
- Invariants Preserved:
  - `AI EXPLAINS — DETERMINISTIC RULES DECIDE.`
  - `THE UNIVERSITY SIS REMAINS AUTHORITATIVE.`
  - `READ-ONLY FIRST.`
  - NO real university connected; NO real student data used; NO real SIS credentials stored; NO production write access.
  - Reference Sandbox: Fake University (`https://fake-university-aqdn.onrender.com`).
  - Local Supabase validated: `http://127.0.0.1:54321` / PostgreSQL port 54322.
- Starting SHA: `20cb3a6b60f126af578e174cd60feb734044c7df`

**Implementation Details:**
1. Database Schema & Migration (`supabase/migrations/20261020120000_pilot_onboarding_system.sql` - 14,477 bytes):
   - Created 7 normalized tables: `university_pilots`, `university_pilot_stakeholders`, `university_pilot_requirements`, `university_pilot_blockers`, `university_pilot_acceptance_checks`, `university_pilot_signoffs`, `university_pilot_activity`.
   - Enabled RLS (`ALTER TABLE ... ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`) on all 7 tables.
   - Revoked all privileges from `anon` and `public`; granted full CRUD to `service_role` and tenant-scoped `SELECT` to `authenticated` users via `institutional_memberships`.
   - Applied cleanly to local Supabase with `npx supabase migration up`.
2. Backend Domain Package (`apps/api/app/pilot_onboarding/`):
   - `models.py` (10,783 bytes): 19-state lifecycle model, Pydantic domain entities, `PilotSummary`, `PilotReport`, `WriteEnablementResult`.
   - `defaults.py` (23,092 bytes): 21 default IT requirements, 21 default acceptance checks, 13-section technical questionnaire, 11-topic security questionnaire, and explicit `DATA_REQUIREMENTS_MATRIX` enforcing data minimization.
   - `state_machine.py` (11,404 bytes): Deterministic transition engine enforcing prerequisites, blocking on active SEV1/SEV2 blockers, validating signoffs, and fail-closed gate evaluations.
   - `write_enablement.py` (9,243 bytes): 12-criteria fail-closed gate checking registration/drop write capabilities, idempotency, zero reconciliation drift, active audit log, SIS health, authoritative window/capacity/conflicts, error contracts, sandbox mutation, and platform admin approval.
   - `acceptance_engine.py` (5,234 bytes): Automated suite evaluation and Markdown report generation.
   - `repository.py` (12,668 bytes): PostgREST client implementation for all 7 pilot tables.
   - `service.py` (24,418 bytes): Complete orchestration of pilots, lifecycle transitions, requirement updates, blocker tracking, signoffs, and audit activity.
   - `__init__.py` (2,011 bytes): Package export declarations.
3. Backend API Routes (`apps/api/app/api/routes/institutional_pilots.py` - 17,128 bytes):
   - 22 REST endpoints under `/api/v1/institutional/pilots/*` covering pilot lifecycle, summaries, requirements, blockers, stakeholders, acceptance suites, automated test execution, write enablement evaluation, signoff recording, reports, and activity logs.
   - Wired router and dependencies in `apps/api/app/main.py` (37,777 bytes).
4. Documentation & Operational Package:
   - 15 comprehensive operational guides in `docs/pilot/first-university/` (executive overview, IT discovery, SIS API specs, SSO identity, data minimization, curriculum mapping, network security, webhooks/sync, sandbox requirements, read-only scope, acceptance criteria, support escalation, go-live readiness, IT handoff, pilot validation runbook).
   - 9 structured CSV templates in `docs/pilot/templates/` (university IT discovery, security questionnaire, faculty/major/plan/course/term mappings, test students, acceptance checklist).
5. Frontend Implementation (`apps/web`):
   - `apps/web/src/lib/api/authenticated-client.ts` (3,346 bytes): Extended `AuthenticatedApiPath` with `/api/v1/institutional/pilots${string}`.
   - `apps/web/src/lib/api/institutional-pilots.ts` (13,766 bytes): Full typed API client with 22 methods using authenticated client request pipeline.
   - `apps/web/src/app/institutional/pilots/page.tsx` (36,327 bytes): Pilot Dashboard with KPI cards, filters, search, templates section, and pilot creation modal.
   - `apps/web/src/app/institutional/pilots/[pilot_id]/page.tsx` (106,861 bytes): Pilot Command Center featuring 10 operational tabs (Overview, Scope, Requirements, Questionnaires, Blockers, Acceptance, Write Enablement Gate, Signoffs, Stakeholders, Activity).
   - `InstitutionalShell.tsx` (3,387 bytes): Added Navigation link to `/institutional/pilots`.
   - `integrations/page.tsx` (31,667 bytes): Added cross-navigation action to `/institutional/pilots`.

**Test Commands & Results:**
- Local Supabase Migration: `npx supabase migration up` -> `Applied migration 20261020120000_pilot_onboarding_system.sql`.
- Focused Backend Unit Tests: `pytest apps/api/tests/test_pilot_onboarding.py -q -rs` -> **17 passed, 0 failed in 1.89s**.
- Real Local Supabase Database Integration Tests: `pytest apps/api/tests/test_pilot_onboarding_local_supabase.py -q -rs` -> **3 passed, 0 failed in 1.21s** (tested real local PostgreSQL insertions, updates, check constraints, cascade deletions, and fail-closed RLS anon privilege revocation).
- Combined Pilot Tests: `pytest apps/api/tests/test_pilot_onboarding.py apps/api/tests/test_pilot_onboarding_local_supabase.py -q -rs` -> **20 passed, 0 failed in 2.56s**.
- Full Backend Regression Suite: `apps\api\.venv311\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest apps/api/tests -q -rs` -> **2,341 passed, 15 failed (known baseline: 4 advisor explanation, 11 CORS), 99 skipped in 91.75s**.
  - `NEW_REGRESSIONS_INTRODUCED = 0`.
- Frontend Focused Unit Tests: `npm test -- "src/app/institutional/pilots/page.test.tsx"` -> **2 passed in 1.35s**.
- Full Frontend Regression Suite: `npm test -- --run` -> **41 test files passed, 250 tests passed, 0 failed in 30.90s**.

- Next.js Production Build: `npm run build` -> **Compiled successfully in 34.1s, static/dynamic routes generated cleanly with exit code 0**.

**Security Checks:**
- Row Level Security (RLS) is enabled on all 7 pilot tables.
- Public and anon access explicitly revoked.
- Fail-closed write enablement: requires all 12 criteria to pass before write pilot mode can be enabled.

- No connection to real universities, real student data, or real SIS credentials.
- No modifications to production Supabase (`lzwttbjnuhdllesfuzzs`).

Direct PostgreSQL ACL inspection returned `prosecdef=true`, `search_path=""`, EXECUTE `PUBLIC=false`, `anon=false`, `authenticated=false`, `service_role=true`. A second clean `supabase db reset --local` again applied all 25 migrations. The combined local audit then yielded **95 passed, 4 failed, 2 warnings in 169.13s**. The four failures were stale fixtures (three diagnostic tests using an unmapped synthetic plan for positive Student Intelligence calls; one university-sync mock with a faculty/major that cannot identify canonical Plan 12), not V2 failures. Full combined-audit rerun remains pending.

**Remaining before release:** Add and execute the explicit V1/V2 A–J idempotency/immutability matrix and direct PostgreSQL privilege proof; rerun the complete local Supabase audit and institutional gates; classify/fix remaining fixture failures; run focused/full backend and web suites, typecheck, build, lint/diff checks. ## Institutional Intelligence Production Phase: Implementation Complete, Release Blocked (2026-10-19)

**Objective:** Audit the existing Institutional Intelligence implementation against the production-phase requirements, preserve accepted deterministic/privacy boundaries, reproduce the three historical in-scope failures, and establish the smallest coherent implementation slice for university/program/course aggregate intelligence across all 17 canonical curricula.

**Starting SHA/branch and worktree:** `3d0c2ec8a9ffb1cf582fd65dce20af795fab26b1` / `main`. The only pre-existing modification was this progress record containing the completed registration production-acceptance entry; it is preserved. No files were staged, committed, pushed, or deployed.

**CURRENT_INSTITUTIONAL_ARCHITECTURE:** The repository already contains (1) a pure P7 institutional signal engine in `app/institutional_intelligence`, (2) an authorized course-at-a-time service in `app/institutional_intelligence_service`, (3) aggregate declared-intent demand from P6 mock registration, (4) deterministic capacity/offering adapters, (5) an institutional natural-language query interpreter that selects allowlisted metrics, and (6) four legacy institutional web surfaces for capacity, cohorts, change impact, and AI query. Existing authority is an active `INSTITUTIONAL_ANALYST` membership; advisor authority is modeled separately as `ACADEMIC_ADVISOR` plus explicit advisor-student assignments.

**REUSABLE_COMPONENTS:** Verified catalog and prerequisite graph models; student academic profiles and attempts; eligibility/progress/degree-path engines; authoritative Fake University offering snapshot contract; registration context; P6 privacy suppression and tenant membership; existing FastAPI auth boundary; existing RTL/Tailwind web design system; deterministic institutional AI interpreter/tool allowlist.

**OBSOLETE_COMPONENTS:** The P7 contract is limited to one course/one plan/one period, primarily measures declared mock-registration intent, uses only the analyst role, and deliberately excludes overview/program/progress/advisor-caseload/simulation capabilities. Legacy institutional pages are independent feature demonstrations rather than one production dashboard. Some policy text prohibits any bottleneck score, while the new approved production specification requires a transparent deterministic score; the new implementation will retain raw factors and reason codes so the score is never opaque.

**MISSING_COMPONENTS:** Batched university/program/course repository, authoritative current-registration rollups, deterministic demand projection components, capacity rollup/status thresholds, progress bands/blocker aggregates, review queue, advisor caseload buckets, non-mutating institutional simulations, unified overview/filter API, all-17 acceptance matrix, and production dashboard navigation/pages.

**MULTI_PLAN_GAPS:** Existing evaluation requires a single `study_plan_id`; it does not roll shared courses across plan keys or attribute demand by program/faculty. Composite curriculum identity must be introduced at the aggregate boundary; Plan Number 12 alone will never be used as identity. Empty/sparse programs need typed insufficient-data/suppression states.

**Baseline test evidence:** `apps/api/.venv311/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest apps/api/tests/test_institutional_intelligence.py apps/api/tests/test_institutional_intelligence_service_api.py apps/api/tests/test_institutional_ai_query.py apps/api/tests/test_institutional_ai_query_api.py apps/api/tests/test_institutional_policy.py apps/api/tests/test_institutional_policy_ingestion.py -q -rs` -> **178 passed, 3 failed, 2 warnings in 12.65s**. The same three historical service tests fail before institutional evaluation in `create_p6_replay_artifact`: the immutable P6 V1 source hash rejects the current validator/dependency source as `ENGINE_VERSION_UNAVAILABLE`. This is an in-scope integration defect and will be resolved by validating and updating the version pin, never by disabling integrity verification.

**Security/data decision:** No DB-to-DB access and no production mutation. Aggregate analytics will use synchronized Morshidi academic state and the existing read-only Fake University offering contract. Default responses will be aggregate-only; raw diagnostic answers and student names/emails are out of contract. No migration is assumed until repository/role analysis proves one necessary.

**Implemented production slice:** Added a typed, batched aggregate repository and pure deterministic analytics engine under `apps/api/app/institutional_analytics`; authenticated aggregate routes under `/api/v1/institutional/analytics`; university/program/course demand and capacity rollups; transparent bottleneck scoring; progress distribution; review queue; advisor caseload buckets; non-mutating section-capacity simulation; and an allowlisted institutional copilot whose model may select a tool but never supplies or changes governed values. Composite curriculum identity is `ZU:{faculty}:{major}:P{plan_number}`. Projected demand is a distinct union of current registration, verified eligible-not-registered students, latest valid mock-registration intent, and one-step verified degree-path need, excluding completed/current courses. Capacity states are deterministic: under-utilized `<50%`, healthy `50–84%`, near capacity `85–100%`, and over demand when projected demand exceeds capacity. The bottleneck score is transparent and bounded at 100: required-course factor 25, downstream dependencies up to 25, unmet-demand share up to 30, and affected-student share up to 20.

**P6 replay compatibility repair:** Preserved the immutable historical replay-engine V1 pin and introduced replay engine V2 with current normalized source hashes. New artifacts use V2 while the intent contract remains V1. Unknown-engine fail-closed tests now use V3. Integrity verification was not disabled or relaxed.

**Frontend:** Added a unified Arabic RTL institutional shell and responsive, accessible pages for overview, programs, courses, bottlenecks, capacity intelligence, progress, review queue, what-if simulation, and copilot. Visual bars have textual/table equivalents; focus states, reduced-motion behavior, and touch targets are preserved. Existing design tokens and Lucide icons are reused.

**Security/privacy:** Access is limited to `INSTITUTIONAL_ANALYST` university scope or `ACADEMIC_ADVISOR` explicit assignment scope. The service authorizes before aggregate reads, emits no names/emails/raw student records, applies sparse program suppression, sets private/no-store responses, and uses the existing read-only HTTP Fake University offering boundary. `DIRECT_DB_TO_FAKE_UNIVERSITY = NO`. No database migration was added. Simulations are pure and non-mutating. Unsafe PII/SQL copilot requests abstain before any provider call. Hidden reasoning and credentials are not returned.

3. **RLS & Privilege Enforcement (Step 6):** Tested against real Supabase roles. `anon` role blocked from direct insert/update/delete on enrollments/audit and blocked from `university_record_registration_audit`. `authenticated` role blocked from bypassing integration boundary. `service_role` allowed. Privileged RPC `university_record_registration_audit` has `PUBLIC`, `anon`, and `authenticated` revoked, granted only to `service_role`, with `SECURITY DEFINER` and `search_path = ''`.
4. **Student Ownership (Step 7):** Scoped integration token claims strictly determine student identity; query param overrides ignored; cross-student drop blocked (`INVALID_SECTION`).
5. **PostgreSQL Idempotency (Step 8):** Identical request with same idempotency key returns cached idempotent result (`duplicate: true`); exactly 1 enrollment created, exactly 1 seat consumed. Different idempotency key returns deterministic `ALREADY_REGISTERED`. Duplicate enrollments/seats consumed = 0.
6. **Critical Final-Seat Concurrency Gate (Step 9 - RELEASE GATE):** Created isolated section with capacity = 1. Fired concurrent registrations from 2 eligible synthetic students over 5 consecutive test iterations. In 100% of runs: exactly 1 student succeeded (`SUCCESS_COUNT = 1`), exactly 1 student rejected (`SECTION_FULL_COUNT = 1`), final enrollment count = 1, available seats = 0, `DOUBLE_BOOKING_OCCURRED = NO`.
7. **Drop & Seat Restoration (Step 10):** Dropping enrollment cleanly decrements enrolled count, increments available seats back to 1, removes enrollment, records audit event. Idempotent drop retry does not double-restore seats.
8. **Preview Non-Mutating (Step 11):** 5 consecutive preview requests verify 0 mutations to enrollments, seats, audit events, or academic records.
9. **Registration Policies on Real Postgres (Step 12):** Deterministic validation executed for: `REGISTRATION_CLOSED`, `SECTION_FULL`, `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT`, `INVALID_SECTION`, `COURSE_NOT_OFFERED`, `NOT_ELIGIBLE`, `REVIEW_REQUIRED` (fails closed), and `CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`.
10. **Audit Trail & Security (Step 13):** Every confirmed and rejected registration/drop recorded in `registration_events`. Zero service credentials, tokens, passwords, or hidden reasoning exposed in payloads.

## End-to-End Course Registration Phase (2026-10-19, implementation complete; release blocked)

**Objective:** Implement the smallest coherent authoritative registration slice across Morshidi and Fake University for all 17 canonical curricula, preserving the SIS API boundary and deterministic academic rules.

**Starting SHAs/branches:** Morshidi `9b4b471d88f3d56b45f4fe26e6a32d964726a132` / `main`; Fake University `1482612c033c1a5816f48b6b415a8664c0f11748` / `main`.

**Verified starting worktrees:** Morshidi had only this pre-existing modified progress record. Fake University had only untracked `supabase/.temp/`, which is preserved.

**Architecture audit checkpoint:** Fake University already owns students, curricula, calendar/registration-window state, offerings, current enrollment embedded in the student profile, idempotent events, SQLite transactions, Supabase persistence, and the `university_register_sections` atomic RPC with calendar/student/offering row locks and optimistic retries. Existing `/v1/me/schedule` is authenticated but replaces the full schedule and lacks authoritative preview, plan-membership enforcement, explicit review-required handling, student integration endpoints, and a dedicated drop contract. Morshidi already has deterministic eligibility, structured offering/conflict logic, mock-registration engines, university identity mapping/sync, and a server-only Fake University client; it has no real registration service/routes/UI. The upstream integration token is deliberately transient during login, so subsequent mutations require a safe server-mediated student-scoped exchange or equivalent without exposing that token to the browser.

**Authoritative data location:** Fake University remains the only enrollment/seat authority. Morshidi shadow academic state may refresh only after confirmed mutation.

**Phase/slice:** Authoritative Fake University registration domain and API, Morshidi server-mediated integration, post-confirmation sync, Arabic-first RTL student registration UI, and deterministic regression coverage.

**Files changed and verified from disk (real sizes):**
- Morshidi backend: `apps/api/app/main.py` (33,831 bytes), `apps/api/app/university_sync/client.py` (26,997), `apps/api/app/university_sync/service.py` (32,609), `apps/api/app/api/routes/registration.py` (4,305), `apps/api/app/registration/__init__.py` (171), `apps/api/app/registration/service.py` (3,389), `apps/api/tests/test_registration_api.py` (2,889), and `apps/api/tests/test_registration_service.py` (2,688).
- Morshidi web: `apps/web/src/lib/api/registration.ts` (3,270), `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/registration/page.tsx` (14,427), `page.test.tsx` (2,931), `apps/web/src/components/navigation/AppShell.tsx` (5,936), and `AppShell.test.tsx` (1,668).
- Fake University: `server/registration/registrationService.ts` (15,006), `server/registrationService.test.ts` (16,278), `server/routes/integrations/morshidi.ts` (23,961), `server/store.ts` (35,264), `server/repositories/universityRepository.ts` (2,908), `sqliteUniversityRepository.ts` (3,411), `supabaseUniversityRepository.ts` (35,254), `src/services/academicEngine.ts` (9,134), `src/types/student.ts` (4,427), and `supabase/migrations/20261019143000_registration_audit_and_demo_offerings.sql` (4,366).
- This progress record is 484,098 bytes before this final update. All expected created files exist and are nonzero.

**Implementation details:**
1. Fake University remains the source of truth. A repository-backed `RegistrationService` now returns authoritative context and plan-filtered offerings, performs preview, registration, and drop, and never lets Morshidi mutate seats locally.
2. The rule path is deterministic: exact composite curriculum identity, plan membership, completed/current course detection, prerequisite AND checks, fail-closed `REVIEW_REQUIRED`, registration window, section state/capacity, duplicate course/section, schedule overlap, and explicit demo-policy 18-credit limit. Multiple independent meeting blocks are supported by the shared conflict engine and both repository mutation paths.
3. Registration uses the existing transactional Fake University schedule mutation. SQLite transactions and the Supabase `university_register_sections` RPC serialize calendar, student, and offering locks; the final seat is rechecked at mutation time. Request idempotency is persisted, and structured `registration.audit` events cover confirmed and rejected register/drop outcomes, including mutation-time race failures.
4. Fake University exposes scoped integration context, offerings, preview, register, and drop endpoints. A client-authenticated server-to-server exchange issues a short-lived student-scoped token. Browser requests cannot supply or override a student identity.
5. Morshidi resolves the authenticated owner through the existing university identity mapping, exchanges credentials only server-side, forwards stable registration operations and idempotency keys, and refreshes the existing shadow profile only after an authoritative success. It never provisions a new identity during refresh.
6. The Arabic-first RTL registration page shows active-term/window/credit context, current schedule and drop controls, searchable plan-safe offerings, requirement group, eligibility and reasons, seat/instructor/room information, all meeting blocks, cart, authoritative preview/blockers, resulting credits, confirmation, accessible focus/status behavior, and responsive layouts. Success is shown only after Fake University confirms.

## Investigation: Student Intelligence Domain Classification & RESULTING_LEVEL Semantics (2026-10-18, complete)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Investigate observed diagnostic output where domain `NURSING_FUNDAMENTALS` with score 81/100 was output with label `MEDIUM`, while domains with scores 51/100, 59/100, and 26/100 were output with label `HIGH`. Determine exact field semantics, the computing code, whether the label is mastery/proficiency or measurement confidence, identify canonical mastery thresholds, and rerun classification without altering engine behavior.

**Starting commit SHA:** `f30070a552f3b24a041ac56e2b69bf54205bd41e`

**Investigation Details & Root Cause Analysis:**
1. **Root Cause:** The `(MEDIUM)` and `(HIGH)` values formatted in the previous delivery summary were sourced from the `confidence` attribute of `DiagnosticDomainResult`, which measures **statistical measurement confidence / observation certainty** (evaluating response completeness, timing reliability, absence of speed-guessing, and inter-half split consistency). It does NOT represent student mastery or proficiency level.
2. **Computing Code:** In `apps/api/app/student_intelligence/nursing_diagnostic.py` (lines 606-611) and `diagnostic.py` (lines 342-347), `confidence` is assigned as `HIGH` if `domain_consistent`, `fast_count == 0`, and no quality flags are present; otherwise `MEDIUM` or `LOW`. For student 209900017, domains scored consistently low in both halves without speed-guessing received `confidence = HIGH` (high measurement certainty of a low score).
3. **Canonical Mastery Representation:** In Morshidi architecture, mastery is quantified by the numeric `score` (0..100) and classified into canonical tiers per `profile_v2.py` (lines 245-247):
   - `STRENGTH`: `score >= 75` (and non-low confidence).
   - `DEVELOPMENT_AREA`: `score < 60`.
   - `COMPETENT`: `60 <= score < 75` (baseline competent range).
4. **Behavioral Integrity:** As instructed, because the label is measurement confidence and not mastery, no code or behavioral changes were made (`BUG_CONFIRMED = NO`, `FIX_REQUIRED = NO`, `FILES_CHANGED = []`).
5. **Rerun & Classification Verification:**
   - `NURSING_FUNDAMENTALS`: Score 81 -> Mastery: `STRENGTH` (Confidence: `MEDIUM`)
   - `ANATOMY_PHYSIOLOGY_FOUNDATIONS`: Score 51 -> Mastery: `DEVELOPMENT_AREA` (Confidence: `HIGH`)
   - `PHARMACOLOGY_FOUNDATIONS`: Score 59 -> Mastery: `DEVELOPMENT_AREA` (Confidence: `HIGH`)
   - `PATIENT_SAFETY_INFECTION_CONTROL`: Score 26 -> Mastery: `DEVELOPMENT_AREA` (Confidence: `HIGH`)
   - `CLINICAL_ASSESSMENT_REASONING`: Score 26 -> Mastery: `DEVELOPMENT_AREA` (Confidence: `HIGH`)

**Acceptance status:** COMPLETE. Zero code changes required; zero database mutations.

The package defines 17 curricula across 7 faculties; every curriculum uses plan number `12`, while canonical `plan_key` values distinguish institution/faculty/major/version (for example `ZU:IT:AI:P12`, `ZU:IT:SE:P12`, `ZU:LAW:LAW:P12`, and `ZU:NURS:NURS:P12`). Existing downstream academic engines predominantly consume `state.study_plan_id`, which is a promising already-generic boundary pending focused audit. **Exact next point:** classify every relevant occurrence in both repositories; inspect Fake University persistence/API/auth boundaries and Morshidi sync, project policy, grade-policy, Student Intelligence, catalog, advisor, and conversation paths; then implement the smallest coherent contract/registry/resolver slice with focused and regression tests.

**Implemented Fake University foundation:** Added strict `CurriculumIdentity`/`CurriculumDefinition` models and `planKey` parser, a persisted local curriculum registry, and SQLite/Supabase repository reads keyed only by the composite plan key. Student records now retain `planKey`, `planNumber`, `facultyCode`, and `majorCode`; the only legacy inference is the exact existing `ZU:IT:AI:P12` fixture. Sync now validates `planKey`, institution, faculty, major, and plan-number consistency, traverses the institution-scoped faculty/major/plan relation, and accepts exactly one match; plan number alone, first-match selection, ambiguity, and unknown identities fail closed with typed errors. Existing academic profiles are reconciled to the resolved `study_plan_id`. Added an explicit curriculum-intelligence registry: only the exact AI Plan 12 has taxonomy/workload mappings, and unsupported curricula receive a typed unsupported response rather than AI-derived behavior. The verified Fake University grade contract is institution-scoped, with exact-plan registry precedence, because its upstream semantics are institution-wide. Existing project-credit logic remains exact AI-plan plus exact course-code logic. The wider catalog, progress, eligibility, recommendation, planner, degree-path, advisor, and conversation engines already consume resolved `study_plan_id`; their generic boundary is retained. ## Conversational Academic Intelligence agent upgrade (2026-10-16, in progress)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Replace the configured OpenAI advisor's mandatory strict intent-JSON bottleneck with a bounded Responses API function-calling loop while preserving every deterministic academic engine as the sole authority. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `38abfb3b82de1e4771f8805fdeda2e762a419981`, equal to `origin/main`; the starting worktree contained only this intentionally unstaged progress record. The implementation is backend-only unless inspection proves a frontend contract change is required. No production resource, migration, Supabase operation, staging, commit, push, or deployment is authorized.

**Verified starting architecture and plan:** `OpenAIAdvisorProvider.interpret()` currently forces every non-pre-routed message through `INTERPRETATION_JSON_SCHEMA`; `_extract_output_text()` accepts only a completed `message/output_text`, so expected `function_call` output becomes `ADVISOR_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_INVALID`. `AdvisorService` then selects one intent and one deterministic orchestration branch, which loses genuine multi-tool composition. Existing owner-scoped conversation history is already bounded to 20 recent snippets and 1,600 characters. Existing progress, eligibility, recommendation, semester-plan, degree-path, course-information, Student Intelligence V2, and ephemeral What-If engines will be reused through typed read-only tools; authenticated owner identity will remain an internal executor argument and will not appear in model tool schemas. The compatibility interpretation/explanation contracts and deterministic pre-router will remain for unconfigured/legacy providers and safe fallbacks, but will not gate configured conversational OpenAI turns. Next: add provider-neutral conversational contracts, a strict tool registry/executor, a five-round agent loop, Responses API multi-item parsing/error handling, service wiring, and semantic/tool-routing regression tests.

**Implemented architecture and security boundary:** Added a provider-neutral five-round conversational loop that accepts ordinary text or one/many Responses API `function_call` items, carries provider output items forward for stateless continuation, executes independent read-only tools concurrently, returns validated `function_call_output` items, and emits a normal final answer. The configured OpenAI provider now uses this path; legacy/unconfigured providers retain the strict interpretation path and pre-router compatibility. Thirteen production tool contracts cover academic summary/progress, bounded Student Intelligence V2, eligibility, eligible courses, adaptive recommendations, course facts/comparison, remaining requirements, semester plan, degree path, current enrollments, and the existing ephemeral What-If engine. Strict Pydantic schemas forbid extra fields and contain no owner/student/institution identifiers; owner and university scope come only from the authenticated service boundary. Student Intelligence uses the bounded projection and never raw answers/keys. What-If resolves canonical course identity, uses the existing adapter/context loader, and performs no persistence. Provider failures use deterministic fallback where safe or a friendly temporary response. Final prose strips internal machine labels, and safe logs retain only tool names/limitations, never prompts, private records, secrets, or hidden reasoning.

**Conversation semantics and scenarios:** The production instruction makes OpenAI the semantic conversation/orchestration layer for Jordanian/Levantine Arabic, MSA, English, mixed language, typos, pronouns, follow-ups, and multiple clauses. General chat takes one provider call and zero academic loads. Student-specific facts require tools; parallel multi-tool results can be combined while the unchanged public response retains one primary deterministic payload. Exact regression coverage for `شو بتعرف عني؟` proves `get_student_intelligence` is requested and the old classifier is not called. Additional tests cover direct general chat, three-tool composition, bounded follow-up reference context, invalid/unknown arguments, tool failure recovery, provider failure, round limit, multiple output items/calls, refusal/incomplete output, identity exclusion, and internal-code redaction. Existing modeled `202610099` coverage proves 30-question diagnostic evidence with zero fabricated official evidence; existing `202310001` coverage proves official fixture evidence remains dominant.

**Validation and baseline comparison:** Initial new tests failed only because `pytest-asyncio` is not installed; switching to the repository-standard AnyIO marker fixed the harness. An initial full run found one new structural failure because the new executor imported FastAPI serialization inside the pure advisor package; replacing it with a local dataclass/enum/decimal serializer removed that regression. Final requested focused matrix: **523 passed, 2 dependency warnings in 14.61s**. Final full backend: **2,163 passed, 28 failed, 96 skipped, 3 warnings in 77.01s**. The 28 failures exactly match the established baseline categories (4 legacy advisor explanation expectations, 11 CORS/settings, 3 institutional intelligence, 5 mock-registration replay, 5 mock-registration service/API); there are **0 new failures**. Python compileall passed. `git diff --check` passed with one informational LF-to-CRLF notice. Secret-pattern scan found none. Local settings reported both advisor key and model unconfigured without printing values, so the real provider probe is **NOT TESTED**. No frontend file changed, so frontend tests/build were not run.

**Files, sizes, acceptance, and exact next point:** Backend implementation/test files: `main.py` 32,687 bytes; `advisor_openai.py` 13,102; `advisor.py` 35,037; `conversation_agent.py` 7,752; `tool_executor.py` 8,169; `tool_registry.py` 5,101; provider tests 11,728; service tests 31,230; conversational-agent tests 10,330. Architecture docs: `advisor-orchestration.md` 15,243 and `advisor-provider-and-explanation.md` 4,754. This required progress record remains modified and includes earlier owner work. All listed files exist and are nonzero. **Acceptance:** CONVERSATIONAL TOOL-CALLING ARCHITECTURE IMPLEMENTED AND BASELINE-GATED; UNCOMMITTED. **Remaining limitation:** no live OpenAI contract probe was possible without configured credentials; grounded policy search is intentionally not exposed as a chat tool because its owner-scoped integration was not part of the established advisor service boundary. **Exact next point:** human review of the unstaged architecture and tests, then a small non-production provider probe in a credentialed environment; any stage, commit, push, deploy, migration, or Supabase action requires separate authorization.

## Frontend numeric contrast and readability audit (2026-10-16, complete)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Frontend-visual-only audit and repair of dark-surface numeric/readability contrast, beginning with the invisible official-evidence `0` and diagnostic-evidence `30` on `/student/intelligence` and extending across all 93 source files under `apps/web/src/app` and `apps/web/src/components`. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `3cd890170d49c4841727d9d52afc4c0c758e48bb`, equal to `origin/main`; the starting worktree contained only this intentionally unstaged progress record. Read the frontend instructions and used the UI/UX contrast guidance (normal dark-surface text at least 4.5:1, context-aware tokens) plus the React multi-component checklist. No backend, Supabase, database, migration, API contract, authentication, academic/diagnostic calculation, displayed value, package, stage, commit, push, or deployment was changed or performed.

**Root causes and audit findings:** The global dark workspace intentionally remaps several legacy light Tailwind backgrounds (`bg-white`, stone/amber/red/emerald 50/100) to dark surfaces, but some descendants retained legacy `text-stone-950/700/600` or old hardcoded dark hex colors. The mismatch—not a zero-value branch—made meaningful numbers and metadata dark-on-dark. The audit searched dark backgrounds, dark neutral/black text, opacity 20–40, arbitrary/inline colors, metric/count/GPA/credit/score/progress/semester/table/badge renderers, SVG/chart text, zero conditionals, shared `StatCard`/dial/path primitives, student routes, and institutional routes. Existing shared dials, path SVG labels, progress components, tables, badges, and most dashboard metrics already inherit high-contrast semantic tokens. Remaining `text-stone-950/700` occurrences after repair are intentionally paired with light amber/stone controls, not dark surfaces.

**Implementation:** Added reusable `.metric-value`, `.metric-value-accent`, and `.metric-value-muted` semantic classes in `globals.css`, including print-safe overrides; made the shared `StatCard` and dial value explicit high-contrast consumers. The Intelligence evidence counts now use primary white and gold, skill scores use gold, and evidence/limitation metadata uses readable semantic secondary text. The active diagnostic counter now uses the primary metric token, and disabled transition/submit actions increased from 40% to 60% opacity. Replaced unsafe legacy dark neutral classes with `text-foreground`, `text-muted`, `text-success`, or metric tokens in institutional AI query/change impact/root shells, student offerings/plan transition/privacy roots, decision-history version/provenance states, and policy metadata/handoff states. ## Diagnostic V2 completion response serialization repair (2026-10-16, complete)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Backend response-boundary-only repair for the production P30 completion defect reported after persistence had already succeeded. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `0e43b1d9b669b6cb6b6f481cc8f6d5db7a10dc6d`, equal to `origin/main`; the starting worktree contained only this pre-existing unstaged progress record. The objective was to make the completed diagnostic response accept the internal immutable Student Intelligence V2 profile and its nested skill dataclasses without changing persistence, deterministic scoring/evidence logic, the migration, frontend behavior, or V1 compatibility. No production resource, Supabase operation, migration, stage, commit, push, or deployment was authorized or performed.

**Root cause and implementation:** The service correctly completed and persisted the V2 answer/results, then attached a real `StudentIntelligenceProfileV2` dataclass to the response payload. `DiagnosticSessionResponse.model_validate(...)` attempted to validate that object through the nested `StudentIntelligenceProfileV2Response`, but that response model and `SkillDimensionV2Response` did not enable attribute-based parsing. The standalone profile route masked the missing nested contract by explicitly passing `from_attributes=True` at its top-level call. Added only `ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)` to those two public response models. No fields, endpoint, route behavior, session lifecycle, scoring, evidence, repository, database, or frontend code changed.

**Regression coverage:** `test_student_intelligence_v2_api.py` now directly validates a real internal profile with a real nested `SkillDimensionV2` inside `DiagnosticSessionResponse`. Its final-answer route fake mirrors P30 completion with status `COMPLETED`, count/maximum 30, no next question, five domain results, and the internal profile; the HTTP response is 200, serializes the nested skill, excludes raw answers/answer keys/correct options/distractor rationale/weights/formulas, and a repeated answer remains 409. The existing standalone GET and non-final answer tests remain in the focused run and pass unchanged. Because the route's established `response_model_exclude_none=True` contract omits a null `question` key, the assertion uses semantic absence-or-null (`body.get("question") is None`) rather than changing the response contract.

**Actual validation:** The historical `apps/api/.venv311` interpreter was absent, global Python 3.14 lacked pytest, and the Anaconda environment lacked FastAPI; these launch/collection failures were not counted as passes. Tests were therefore run in an isolated Python 3.11 environment from `apps/api/requirements.txt`. An initial exact-file run was **4 passed / 1 failed** solely because the regression expected an explicit JSON null despite the existing exclude-none response contract; after correcting the assertion, the exact API file passed **5/5 with 2 dependency warnings**. The final focused schema/diagnostic/intelligence selection passed **44/44 with 2 dependency warnings in 2.80s**. The full backend suite completed **2,146 passed, 28 failed, 96 skipped, 3 warnings in 110.46s**. The exact 28 failures remain confined to the established baseline categories: 4 Advisor explanation, 11 CORS/settings, 3 institutional intelligence, 5 mock-registration replay, and 5 mock-registration service/API; there is no new diagnostic or serialization failure.

**Files, security, recovery, acceptance, and exact next point:** Changed `apps/api/app/api/schemas/student_intelligence_v2.py` (2,967 bytes) and `apps/api/tests/test_student_intelligence_v2_api.py` (10,498 bytes), plus this required pre-existing progress record. No internal evidence fields were added to public schemas. **Acceptance:** RESPONSE SERIALIZATION REPAIR IMPLEMENTED AND REGRESSION-GATED; UNCOMMITTED. **Exact next point:** complete final whitespace, secret, scope, file-size, and Git-status checks, then return the unstaged repair for human review; any stage, commit, push, or deployment requires separate authorization.

## Diagnostic V1 transition UX correction (2026-10-16, complete)

## Diagnostic V1/V2 expand-deploy-contract compatibility bridge (2026-10-16, complete)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Convert the uncommitted Quick Diagnostic V2 migration and application boundary into a zero-break expand/deploy/contract transition while preserving the reviewed V2 behavior. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `b7a9e06c2b03d2ed2c396f62ca6e1c5f55ec80e5`. Starting worktree contains the 31-path Academic Intelligence implementation, including this pre-existing progress record; all work is being preserved. No production resource, staging, commit, push, or deployment is authorized.

**Compatibility audit and selected boundary:** Confirmed the migration currently removes the deployed nine-argument V1 RPC, and confirmed the new and old HTTP answer bodies are mutually incompatible. Also confirmed the deployed frontend requires `question.domain`, so merely making timing/confidence optional would not preserve the old client for newly created V2 sessions. The transition design will keep the existing status/start endpoints V1-by-default for unversioned deployed clients, make the new frontend explicitly request V2, prefer an existing active V1 session without conversion, serialize V1 questions/results with their original policy, and choose the RPC solely from the stored `assessment_version`. V2 timing/confidence will remain mandatory after authoritative session lookup; V1-supplied timing/confidence will not be persisted as genuine evidence. Next: implement both RPC overloads, restore the exact V1 engine under an isolated module, add service/API/repository branching and rollout tests, then replay Local Supabase and run all requested gates.

**Implemented bridge:** The upgrade migration is now expand-only: it uses `CREATE OR REPLACE` for the original nine-argument V1 RPC, adds an explicit V1-version guard, creates the eleven-argument V2 overload beside it, and revokes/grants each signature independently so only `service_role` may execute either. The original V1 bank, adaptive 10–16 selection, scoring, reason codes, question serialization, and policy constants are preserved verbatim in `diagnostic_v1.py`. Repository methods emit signature-specific payloads. The service resumes an active V1 session without conversion, dispatches exclusively from the stored session version, ignores optional timing/confidence for V1, requires both for V2, and never mixes evidence. Unversioned status/start requests remain V1 for the deployed client; the new frontend explicitly requests V2 in both calls. V1 responses retain `domain` for the deployed UI while V2 responses continue omitting it. Future cleanup is documented but deliberately not implemented.

**Database and compatibility evidence:** Revalidated that all Supabase endpoints were localhost-only, then ran pinned CLI `2.117.0` with `db reset --local --no-seed`; the complete migration chain through `20261016090000` replayed successfully. The final real Local Supabase diagnostic suite passed **7/7** with two dependency warnings. It proves both overloads resolve for `service_role`, V1 rejects V2, V2 rejects V1, anon/authenticated calls are denied, owner mismatch and duplicate concurrency fail closed, a stored active V1 session resumes and completes through the new backend, completed V1 results remain readable, and V1 rows retain default database values without acquiring V2 evidence semantics. The final application/static rollout matrix passed **26/26**, and the wider Student Intelligence/Advisor selection passed **106/106**.

## University login cold-start null-GPA contract fix (2026-10-13)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Fixed only the production university-sync cold-start contract so a legitimate student with no completed grade history can authenticate and synchronize without fabricating GPA evidence. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `f95c7c555e048e8907fbe3ddae5baa3b0927f683`. Starting status contained only the pre-existing unstaged `docs/CODEX_PROGRESS.md` modification; it was preserved. Inspected the sync models/client/service/auth route, the academic-profile/attempt schema migration, university/student-intelligence policies, and focused tests before changing behavior. No Fake University data, student `202610099`, Supabase data, migration, production resource, frontend, deployment, stage, commit, push, merge, or pull request was changed or performed.

**Confirmed cause and implementation:** `UniversityGradesResponse.cumulative_gpa` required a strict number, `UniversityGradesSnapshot.cumulative_gpa` required `Decimal`, and `get_student_grades()` unconditionally called `strict_decimal`, so the valid authoritative `cumulativeGpa: null` response became `UniversityProtocolError` and the login route returned `UNIVERSITY_UNAVAILABLE`. The wire response now accepts only a strict numeric GPA or explicit null while still requiring the field; the snapshot retains `Decimal | None`; the client preserves null and parses every numeric value exactly as before. Top-level null GPA is omitted only from bounds parsing; semester GPAs, course numeric grades, the exact `FAKE_UNIVERSITY_GRADING_POLICY_V1` structure/version, and the exact numeric `gpaScale=100` policy match remain strict. Cross-endpoint validation now requires profile and grades GPA presence/absence to match, rejects either asymmetric null case and unequal numeric values, preserves exact numeric/bounds checks, and still validates scales before GPA absence. `_sync_academic_profile` was verified unchanged: because the database schema requires the GPA pair atomically, upstream `(null, 100)` is stored as `(null, null)`, never `0`; zero grade rows create no PASSED/FAILED attempts and five enrollments create five IN_PROGRESS attempts. The strict grouped `student/courses` contract already supports empty completed plus five current rows; no separate course-contract defect was demonstrated or modified.

**Tests and actual outcomes:** Focused command used the requirements-matching Python 3.11 environment at `D:\imporant\Morshidi-main\apps\api\.venv311`. The first attempted command did not start because this checkout's historical `apps/api/.venv311` path is absent. The first executed focused run produced **107 passed, 6 failed, 2 warnings**; the six failures exposed legacy adapters that fabricated GPA `93.6` for empty histories or omitted matching profile GPA for synthetic grade rows. After correcting those fixtures, the next run produced **111 passed, 2 failed, 2 warnings** and identified two remaining inconsistent synthetic fixtures. The final focused university client/service/login plus Student Intelligence cold-start and established-student command completed **113 passed, 0 failed, 2 warnings in 2.77s**. Coverage includes explicit-null grades parsing and snapshot retention, exact scale rejection (`None`, `4`, `0`, and string `"100"`), full `authenticate_and_sync_student` with zero grades/five enrollments, HTTP 200 university login, null profile persistence, IN_PROGRESS-only attempts, both asymmetric null mismatches, different numeric GPA, scale mismatch, unchanged `93.6/100` behavior, and `COLD_START_NO_PERFORMANCE_EVIDENCE`. Full backend `python -m pytest apps/api/tests -q -rs` completed **2,139 passed, 28 failed, 95 skipped, 3 warnings in 82.25s**. Comparing exact node-ID sets to the clean sibling checkout baseline returned `FEATURE_ONLY_FAILURES=0` and `BASELINE_ONLY_FAILURES=0`; the unchanged failures remain 4 Advisor prerequisite routing, 11 CORS/settings, 3 institutional-intelligence, 5 mock-registration replay, and 5 mock-registration service/API nodes. All 95 local-Supabase tests skipped because local-only variables were absent; no database/RLS/RPC execution claim is made.

**Verification, files, security, and acceptance:** Changed `apps/api/app/university_sync/models.py` (19,964 bytes), `client.py` (23,364), `service.py` (26,115), `apps/api/tests/test_university_sync_client_envelopes.py` (22,489), `test_university_sync_service.py` (51,220), and `test_university_login_route.py` (47,503), plus this required progress record. `compileall` passed for all changed Python paths, and `git diff --check` passed. ## Advisor Student Intelligence V2 composition gap (2026-10-12)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Implemented the smallest local-only Advisor composition slice that answers Student Intelligence self-understanding questions and personalizes only the already-determined eligible course set. Exact Prompt B was deterministically pre-routed to `COURSE_RECOMMENDATIONS`; Phase 7 returned the correct legal set; the recommendation template returned immediately before V2/adaptive loading, so the second clause was discarded. Neither production prompt called the interpretation LLM because the pre-router matched, and neither called the explanation LLM because of the early templates.

Production wiring now injects the existing owner-scoped V2 and adaptive loaders. The adaptive projection compares the Phase 7 course-code set with the adaptive set for exact equality and rejects any missing, added, or `eligible=false` course before prose generation. The Advisor system instruction now states that modeled intelligence is not official transcript fact, official evidence is primary, diagnostics secondary, preferences are not mastery, and eligibility cannot be changed.

**Production-like acceptance:** The repository's real modeled `202310001` fixture was exercised with 32 verified official grades, GPA `93.6/100`, 96 earned credits, five current courses, the complete 68-course taxonomy/catalog, Phase 6/7, V2 fusion, and adaptive ranking. ## Student Intelligence V2 final blocker hardening — static validation complete (2026-10-12)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Fixed the three owner-review blockers before database runtime validation: taxonomy integrity, diagnostic RPC state-machine enforcement, and the explicit upstream GPA/grade-policy boundary. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `71d3ba68565a207414ec91ef0617b736a9c9b603`. The pre-existing unstaged Student Intelligence V2 tree was preserved. No migration was executed, Local Supabase was not started, and nothing was staged, committed, pushed, deployed, or written to a remote resource.

**Taxonomy:** The reported `SkillId.LINEAR_ALGEB` typo did **not** exist in the actual source; course `1505366` already used `SkillId.LINEAR_ALGEBRA`. Replaced the count-only import guard with validation against a separate exact 68-code Plan 12 contract. Import now rejects duplicate course codes, missing skills, non-`SkillId` values, duplicate skills within a course, weights outside `(0, 1]`, missing canonical codes, and unexpected codes. Tests compare both the contract and runtime mapping to the canonical seed and statically prohibit course-name inference primitives.

**Diagnostic SQL state machine (migration remains UNAPPLIED):** The atomic `record_student_diagnostic_answer` RPC now verifies stored response count before incrementing; rejects completion before answer 10 and any answer after 16; forces completion on answer 16; rejects the current or any previously answered next question; forbids `p_results` during continuation and requires a five-item result array on completion; requires five unique closed-vocabulary domains; validates numeric fields before casting; bounds per-domain response counts to 1–5; requires `reason_codes` to be an array of 2–4 unique entries from the engine's declared closed vocabulary with matching `RESPONSES_n` and baseline codes; compares every result count to persisted session/domain responses after inserting the current answer; and requires the five counts to sum to `next_order`. Any exception occurs inside the single migration/RPC transaction, so the current answer and partial result rows roll back together. Browser-role revokes, service-role-only execution, owner lock, empty `search_path`, and existing RLS boundaries remain unchanged. Database/RLS/RPC behavior is still **STATICALLY REVIEWED, NOT DATABASE-VERIFIED** because execution was forbidden.

**GPA and governed grade-policy boundary:** The strict upstream contract propagates explicit `gpaScale`. Regression tests prove `93.6` with scale `100` persists without conversion. A GPA received without scale retains no invented scale at the client boundary and persists neither half of the pair under the existing database integrity constraint; it never becomes 4. Added `GradePolicyRegistry`, keyed by exact institution + study plan and validating version, provenance, numeric minimum, and numeric maximum. `StudentService` resolves policies only through this registry; production startup supplies no policy, so Fake University is not activated by identity. With no configured policy, official numeric grades create no mastery and V2 limitations include `official_grade_mastery=UNAVAILABLE_UNVERIFIED_POLICY`; diagnostic intelligence remains available.

**Phase/slice and objective:** Correct the Graduation Path presentation so its primary workload uses the university-reported earned-credit total while preserving the deterministic plan model for course sequencing. ## Student AI chat production recovery — history-independent runtime (2026-10-05)

**Phase/slice and objective:** Focused production/local diagnosis and implementation for the student-only AI chat at `/student/advisor`. Restore usable authenticated chat when durable conversation history is unavailable, preserve deterministic academic authority, retain persisted threads when storage exists, and do not add any human-advisor role/workflow. No commit, push, deploy, production mutation, migration application, or Supabase startup/reset occurred.

**Starting repository state:** `docs/CODEX_PROGRESS.md` was read before implementation and its chat/migration claims were checked against the filesystem and runtime. This supplied directory has no `.git` directory, so branch, starting SHA, worktree status, staged state, and an exact Git diff are **UNVERIFIABLE IN THIS WORKSPACE COPY**; the prior progress claim of an unapplied `20261001200504_add_student_conversation_history.sql` migration was therefore not accepted as production evidence by itself. Existing source, route registration, API client/auth handling, migration, policy documentation, and focused tests were inspected before edits. `apps/web/AGENTS.md` was followed; its expected bundled Next.js docs were absent before `npm ci`, so no bundled documentation was available during the initial inspection.

**Exact production root cause evidence:** The production web route returns `307` to `/login?returnTo=%2Fstudent%2Fadvisor` without a session; API `GET /health` returned `200`; unauthenticated `GET https://morshidi.onrender.com/api/v1/me/conversations` correctly returned `401 AUTH_REQUIRED`; and the CORS preflight for `https://morshidi.vercel.app` returned `200` with the origin, authorization header, and GET/POST methods allowed. The production frontend bundle contains the expected Render API and Supabase public configuration. Using only its public publishable client configuration (never printed or stored), read probes for `student_conversation_threads`, `student_conversation_messages`, and `student_conversation_preferences` each returned **HTTP 404 / PostgREST `PGRST205`**, proving those relations are absent from the deployed schema cache. The authenticated page's first request is `GET /api/v1/me/conversations?offset=0`; the backend adapter converts the missing-relation response to `ConversationUnavailable`, and the registered exception handler returns **HTTP 503 `{ "detail": "Conversation storage unavailable" }`**. The prior frontend coupled that history-only failure to a page-wide `historyError`, rendered the fatal `FriendlyState`, and disabled `ChatComposer`. Production authenticated capture was not possible because no production user/session was available, but the deployed schema response, exact backend conversion/handler, focused 503 route test, and observed UI state converge on this exact request/status path.

**Implementation:** The chat page now models `initializing`, `loading`, `ready`, `error`, and `auth-error` history states separately from `loading`, ordinary send failure, and timeout send failure. History load/detail/pagination errors are non-fatal: the page shows `تعذّر تحميل المحادثات السابقة. يمكنك بدء محادثة جديدة.`, keeps the composer usable, and provides a real history retry without reloading the page. When persistence is healthy, create/continue/list/reload still use owner-scoped `/api/v1/me/conversations` endpoints unchanged. When history is unavailable, sends use the already-existing authenticated student endpoint `POST /api/v1/me/advisor`; general chat remains the provider's fast `GENERAL_CHAT` route and academic requests continue through the deterministic advisor service, with the LLM limited to routing/explanation. No fake response, hard-coded student, auth bypass, tenant relaxation, human-advisor permission, database fallback write, or academic decision logic was added. Authentication expiry is fatal and disables sending; send failures and 55-second timeouts are distinct, clear pending state, retain the exact failed input for a real retry, and never expose raw exceptions/database details. Styling adds compact non-blocking history/send alerts without redesigning the chat.

**Database/migration status:** `supabase/migrations/20261001200504_add_student_conversation_history.sql` exists (7,444 bytes) and defines the three missing tables plus scope triggers, indexes, grants, and owner-read RLS. It is **required for durable production history** but no longer required for current non-persistent chat runtime after this frontend change. It was **not applied locally or to production**. The local Supabase health probe returned `HTTP 000`, no Morshidi Supabase containers were running, and no local migration history could be queried; local application status is therefore **NOT VERIFIED / STACK OFFLINE**. Production absence is verified by three `PGRST205` responses. Apply only after separate human approval, linked-project verification, dry-run review, backup/change-window controls, and live RLS integration testing.

**Tests and verification:** Initial focused runs exposed only test-harness expectations/types and were corrected; they are not represented as passes. Final frontend run `npm run test -- "src/app/(student)/student/advisor/page.test.tsx" "src/app/(student)/student/portal-pages.test.tsx"`: **33 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped in 6.41s**. Coverage includes successful empty load, non-fatal history failure, usable new chat without history, real history retry, general fallback routing, academic deterministic response metadata, persisted create/continue/reload, auth expiry, ordinary send failure without invented output, timeout cleanup, send retry, and absence of a human-advisor endpoint. `npm run typecheck`: **passed**. Focused ESLint on the page/test: **0 errors, 0 warnings** after cleanup. Production-mode `npm run build` with process-only local placeholder public configuration: **passed**, Next.js 16.3.5 compiled and prerendered all 28 pages including `/student/advisor`. Backend run via `uv run --python 3.11 --with-requirements apps/api/requirements.txt python -m pytest apps/api/tests/test_p156_conversations.py apps/api/tests/test_advisor_service.py apps/api/tests/test_advisor_interpretation.py -q -rs`: **131 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 2 dependency deprecation warnings in 5.87s**. Existing backend coverage proves general chat does not load academic catalogs, unsafe academic claims cannot remain general chat, academic requests use authoritative context, owner/tenant isolation remains closed, and storage failures return safe 503 responses. `npm ci` reported 6 dependency audit findings (5 high, 1 critical); no force upgrade was attempted because it is outside this slice.

Modified implementation/tests contain no service-role credential, password, private key, hard-coded student, page reload masking, or human-advisor route/permission (the sole `human-advisor` text is a negative test assertion). Authenticated API/token refresh behavior is unchanged. The fallback endpoint derives the student solely from the bearer token and retains existing deterministic-authority guards. No live database/RLS claim is made without a local or approved production integration run.

## P16 Sandbox Integration — Contributor University Integrated & Verified (2026-10-02)

**P16 IMPLEMENTATION = READY FOR OWNER REVIEW.** Starting and current HEAD: `ca52a894850547d6983e6053f14d879bdee1746b` on main. Objective: Integrate the authoritative contributor Sandbox University (`morshidi-uni`) into the Morshidi Academic Intelligence Platform (`Morshidi`) without rebuilding it, adhering strictly to contract-driven architecture, isolated synthetic tenant boundaries, zero credential leakage, and full test verification.

**Architectural Decision & Data Contract:**
The contributor portal at `d:\morshidi-uni` is the authoritative source of truth for synthetic demo data. Deterministic export script `scripts/export_sandbox_contract.cjs` generates static JSON contracts in `public/api/v1/`: `manifest.json` (schema `1.0.0`, fixture `2026.10.02.v1`), `students.json` (5 personas), `courses.json` (68 courses), `offerings.json` (204 sections), and `academic-records.json` (5 records with semesters and attempts). Static snapshots were mirrored to `apps/api/app/p16_sandbox/fixtures/`. Zero runtime server coupling or cross-repo build dependencies are introduced.

**Bounded Synthetic Tenant (P13 Reuse):**
The sandbox tenant is registered in `apps/api/app/p16_sandbox/tenant.py` with `institution_id = "morshidi-sandbox"`, `display_name = "جامعة مرشدي — البيئة التجريبية"`, country `JO`, locale `ar`, timezone `Asia/Amman`, and theme `sand`. Provider bindings are explicitly bound to `morshidi-sandbox`. Sandbox provider fallback for real institutions is strictly forbidden by `assert_sandbox_institution()`.

**P15 SIS Read Adapter & P10 Offering Provider:**
- `SandboxSISAdapter` implements `SISReadAdapter`, supporting `Entity.STUDENT`, `Entity.COURSE`, `Entity.ATTEMPT`, `Entity.OFFERING`. Bounded pagination, healthy diagnostic reporting (`Freshness.FRESH`), and canonical academic record mapping (`CanonicalSISAcademicRecord`, `StudentCourseAttempt`, outcomes `PASSED`, `FAILED`, `WITHDRAWN`, `Decimal` earned credits).
- `SandboxOfferingProvider` implements `CourseOfferingProvider`, mapping all 204 sections with valid ISO weekdays (1–7), start/end times (`starts_at < ends_at`), `Asia/Amman` timezone, `Modality.IN_PERSON`, capacity, enrolled, and student visibility. Requests for non-sandbox universities return `None` (fail-closed tenant isolation).

**Transport Abstraction & Drift Detection:**
`SandboxUniversityTransport` Protocol abstracts data loading with `StaticFixtureTransport` (local/CI) and `HTTPReadOnlyTransport` (connected demo). `validate_sandbox_contract()` detects version drift, schema violations, count mismatches, and scans recursively for forbidden credential keys (`password`, `token`, `secret`, `credential`).

**Critical Security Invariants:**
- `sandbox_student` persona hint is strictly a DEMO NAVIGATION HINT, NEVER authentication or authorization override.
- `resolve_sandbox_persona()` validates against `ALLOWED_PERSONA_IDS = {"202310001", "202410002", "202410003", "202510004", "202610005"}`. Any persona hint on a non-sandbox institution raises `SandboxPersonaSecurityError` (403 Forbidden). Unknown persona IDs raise `SandboxPersonaNotFoundError` (404 Not Found).
- `sanitize_persona_view()` removes any credentials and decorates responses with `synthetic: True` and explicit watermark `"SYNTHETIC_SANDBOX_PERSONA — NOT PRODUCTION IDENTITY"`.
- Sandbox reset (`POST /api/v1/sandbox/reset` and portal UI "إعادة ضبط البيئة التجريبية") clears demo state with zero writes to production databases or other tenants.

**WC-050 Evidence & WC-053 Observability:**
- `get_wc050_manifest()` provides deterministic tracking of capabilities WC-001 through WC-053 and verification states (`IMPLEMENTED`, `SYNTHETIC_VERIFIED`, `EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY`).
- `SandboxObservability` logs structured events with correlation IDs, latency tracking, health status, and runbook indexing, while strictly masking student IDs (`mask_student_id`) and recording zero PII or credentials.

**Verification Results:**
- P16 test suite `apps/api/tests/test_p16_sandbox.py` (42 tests): **42 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 2.64s**.
- Selected regression suites (185 tests across P13, P15, rules, progress, P15.6): **185 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 3.54s**.
- All files verified from disk: non-zero byte sizes, valid syntax, zero unapproved changes.
- Git status: zero staged files, zero commits, zero pushes, zero migrations applied.
- Next step: Post-P16 final validation register and human review.
